## Open questions

### Q1

What must a header drag do when columnGroups is set?

- Axes: Write the drag into the groups (within-group order, group block order) vs. drag only ungrouped columns among themselves vs. drag a group as a block from any member.
- What each answer changes: The first makes header drag edit groups and fire the groups onValueChange; the second shrinks the draggable set in useGridReorder; the third adds a block-move path in the committer.
- Answer: Write the drag into the groups: a drag reorders inside a group or moves a group block, the drag sticks, and the groups onValueChange fires. (Charlie, 2026-10-26, "All 26 mediums, my answers".)

### Q2

Does the FileUpload family take `readOnly` (prop and Control cascade), or only the Form lock?

- Axes: Public readOnly prop vs lock only; refuse at the handlers vs make the native triggers inert like lockedProps.
- What each answer changes: A public prop changes the props types and the docs surface index (CLAUDE.md §3.4); lock-only stays internal.
- Answer: No new public readOnly prop. Honor the Form lock and the Control readOnly cascade, and make the native triggers inert as Button's lockedProps do. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q3

How does the ChatPrompt send control keep the focus when it cannot send?

- Axes: Native `disabled` (today) against an aria-disabled control that cancels its activation, as Button's lockedProps do; or keep native `disabled` and move the focus to the textarea when the focused control becomes disabled.
- What each answer changes: aria-disabled: the button stays in the tab order and focused, the test at chat-prompt.test.tsx:156 changes from toBeDisabled to aria-disabled. Focus move: the disabled state and its test stay, and the focus lands in the textarea after each send or stop.
- Answer: aria-disabled that cancels the activation, as Button's lockedProps do; the button stays focusable. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q4

On a pie or donut, must ArrowUp and ArrowDown walk the slices, or must the TSDoc name Left and Right only?

- Axes: Behavior (both arrow pairs step slices) vs. contract (keep the engine mapping, and narrow the PieChart and DonutChart TSDoc).
- What each answer changes: Behavior: S11 runs as written. Contract: S11 becomes a TSDoc change and the row drops to correctness.
- Answer: Behavior: ArrowUp and ArrowDown walk the slices, as the TSDoc says. S11 runs as written. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q5

How should an in-grid copy of text that starts with an apostrophe and a formula lead round-trip?

- Axes: Guard such text again on copy (the clipboard then carries `''=1`, which a spreadsheet shows as `'=1`) versus leave copy alone and accept the ambiguity in unguardField (and change nothing).
- What each answer changes: Guard again: S13 edits tsvField and the round trip holds. Accept: G1-C06 is declined and the unguardField TSDoc sentence stays untrue for this input.
- Answer: Guard such text again on copy, so the paste gives back what the user typed. S13 runs as written. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q6

Which menu should Shift+F10 on a focused row open in row roving mode?

- Axes: The cell menu of the row's first data cell, a row-level menu, or none (keep the native menu and record the gap).
- What each answer changes: Cell or row menu: S14 runs with that target. None: G2-C01 is declined as a known gap.
- Answer: The cell menu of the row's first data cell, the menu that a right-click opens there. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q7

Should expanding a client group re-measure the columns, given that it reflows widths under the user?

- Axes: Re-measure on expand (widths may jump) versus count rested leaves as unmeasured at mount only versus accept truncation until Auto-size.
- What each answer changes: Each changes only the trigger S15 keys on, or declines G2-C07.
- Answer: open.

### Q8

What does Enter do on a roving item of a grid with only double-click handlers?

- Axes: Enter fires the double-click handler; roving stands down for double-click only; or keep it and document the gap.
- What each answer changes: First: bridges and the row keydown take the double-click handlers. Second: use-grid-roving.ts mode excludes double-click-only grids and the focus ring goes. Third: TSDoc only.
- Answer: Enter fires the double-click handler. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q9

How should an aggregate row treat a frozen column that falls inside its leading label span (a left-pinned column sits at index 0, so the colSpan label cell covers it)?

- Axes: End the label span at the frozen boundary vs. make the whole label cell sticky vs. leave the label span as it is.
- What each answer changes: Ending the span changes aggregateLabelSpan and the label-span tests; a sticky label cell needs a combined offset for a spanning cell; leaving it scopes S18 to the cells after the span.
- Answer: End the label span at the frozen boundary. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q10

Under the Form lock, does an interactive Rating keep its radios or swap to the display form?

- Axes: Keep radios with aria-readonly during the lock vs swap and restore focus.
- What each answer changes: Keep: rating.tsx branches on readOnly-from-lock apart from readOnly-from-prop. Swap: a focus note and restore around the swap.
- Answer: Keep the radios with aria-readonly during the lock, so the focus stays. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q11

After an Activity hide, do CopyButton and HoldButton reset to rest, or resume the copied window?

- Axes: Reset on cleanup vs re-arm the timer on re-show.
- What each answer changes: Reset: clear refs and state in cleanup. Resume: store the deadline and re-arm in the effect.
- Answer: Reset to rest on cleanup. (Charlie, 2026-10-26.)

### Q12

At the spark tier, where does the touch menu button render when the header becomes the veil?

- Axes: Corner button over the veil; no menu at spark (and say so in the TSDoc); a veil that shows the action.
- What each answer changes: Corner: S30 keys `corner` on the inline header and C2-C06 closes in code. No menu: S30 changes only the TSDoc of place and chartChrome, and C2-C06 becomes documented. Either way the contextMenu TSDoc sentence of C2-C08 changes.
- Answer: An opted-in menu button on a chart whose header is a veil renders in the corner, over the veil (#3112, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q13

Should a picking map with a readout name an unmatched region on the cursor, or stay silent as the readout does?

- Axes: The no-readout picker rule (map-plat.tsx:774-776, 1216-1217) against the readout silence the keyboard tests pin.
- What each answer changes: Name it: S36 runs. Stay silent: M2-C07 is refuted and the 774 comment is narrowed to say so.
- Answer: open.

### Q14

Should QueryBuilder seed one rule when requireRule meets an empty tree, or is the seed the job of the consumer?

- Axes: Seed in the component (uncontrolled seed, or an onValueChange for a controlled empty tree) against a consumer duty that the TSDoc states; the grid column filter already seeds its draft.
- What each answer changes: Seed: a controlled consumer gets a change on mount. Consumer duty: no behavior change; the requireRule TSDoc states that it keeps an existing rule.
- Answer: open.

### Q15

What does FormErrorSummary draw for a field whose issue is ''?

- Axes: Skip the row; draw the row with a fallback name (the field label or a catalog message); or forbid '' in SubmitResult.
- What each answer changes: Skip: the summary can open with no rows, so `visible` stays false for an all-empty failure. Fallback: a new catalog message or a label lookup. Forbid: a type and TSDoc change to SubmitResult, which engine/types.ts:71 now documents as an issue.
- Answer: open.

### Q16

Under tooltip trigger 'click', does a second click dismiss on the same category or only on the same mark?

- Axes: Band-level toggle (the hook's TSDoc and code) against mark-level toggle (the public TSDoc).
- What each answer changes: Band: S41 edits the public TSDoc only. Mark: S41 changes the toggle to compare the pinned mark and keeps the public TSDoc.
- Answer: Keep the per-category toggle and change the public TSDoc to match (#3108, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q17

What should a value pin on the side opposite the data do?

- Axes: Clamp the data to the pinned edge with a minimal non-empty domain; ignore a pin that collapses the domain; widen the other bound to a nice step past the pin.
- What each answer changes: Each gives a different S44 rule and a different domain for the same input; all three remove the midpoint draw.
- Answer: open.

### Q18

Does axes={false} drop the value gridlines as well as the axes, and across the whole chart family (cartesian and point charts)?

- Axes: Gridlines are axis chrome, so bare marks mean no gridlines, as at spark (cartesian and point charts alike); gridlines are a separate layer that the per-axis grid flag owns; the point charts drop them and the cartesian charts keep them.
- What each answer changes: Chrome: S47 gates the grid on draw in buildGrid and in the scatter-chart grid flags, and C1-C10 and C3-C04 close in code. Separate layer: S47 changes the axes TSDoc of both families and adds a way to drop the grid with axes off. Point charts only: S47 runs the scatter edit only, and C1-C10 needs the separate-layer TSDoc change for the cartesian family.
- Answer: open.

### Q19

Clear the probe in the shared RangeLegend, or only in the heatmap wrapper?

- Axes: Shared fix (range-legend.tsx, also reaches the choropleth's MapRangeLegend) vs. heatmap-only fix (heatmap-chart-legend.tsx).
- What each answer changes: Shared: one cleanup closes the heatmap row and any choropleth twin, and the step then needs a choropleth test too. Heatmap-only: the choropleth stays as it is, and the lead stays open.
- Answer: One shared fix in RangeLegend clears the probe on unmount (#3112, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q20

Fix the zero-width demand in minColumns, or only for the missing-widget tile?

- Axes: Engine (minColumns(0, ...) = 1, which also changes JSX tiles with minWidth={0}) vs. spec-tile only (dashboard-tiles.tsx).
- What each answer changes: Engine: one change covers every zero-width tile, and the projection tests need a review. Spec-tile only: the JSX case stays as it is.
- Answer: open.

### Q21

Where does the focus go when edit mode ends under a focused grip or splitter?

- Axes: The board section (tabIndex -1, as in handOffFocus) vs. the first focusable control of the same tile (for example the expand control) vs. the tile's own heading.
- What each answer changes: Board: smallest change, and it matches the fallback that the remove route uses. A control in the tile: keeps the place on the board, but needs a tile at rest with a focusable control.
- Answer: Focus moves to the board when the focused grip or splitter unmounts (#3111, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q22

Does a row-action discard under scope 'cell' mean the whole row, held refusals included, or only the active cell as Escape does?

- Axes: Row-action discard drops held drafts versus it matches Escape (active cell only) and the GridRowActionsContext.discard TSDoc narrows.
- What each answer changes: Whole row: S62 runs. Active cell only: G1-C09 is declined.
- Answer: open.

### Q23

When a seeded width sits below the measured header floor, which one wins?

- Axes: The seed (bounds drop to it) versus the floor (the seed lifts at fit time).
- What each answer changes: Seed: S65 lowers min in resizeBounds/nudge. Floor: S65 lifts the seed in keptWidth and the separator reports a consistent value.
- Answer: open.

### Q24

Should context.chooseColumns open a toolbar-only manager's dialog from a builder item, or be undefined there?

- Axes: Open the mounted dialog versus make the field optional (public type change).
- What each answer changes: Open: S66 edits grid-menu-actions.ts only. Optional: S66 changes the public type and its TSDoc and surface index.
- Answer: open.

### Q25

Does columnFilters.manual count toward the table-wide mode when no column is filterable?

- Axes: Ignore the flag without a filter surface, or keep it and warn.
- What each answer changes: Ignore: resolveFilterMode gates columnManual on hasColumnFilters and the search filters client-side. Keep: only the warning gate changes.
- Answer: open.

### Q26

Should a client search over an empty source keep the header live?

- Axes: hasData from the query text, or only from a manual (server) narrowing.
- What each answer changes: Text: the TSDoc changes, not the code. Manual only: resolveActionable needs the filter mode.
- Answer: open.

### Q27

Under client pagination, does an open detail panel count as a row of the full set?

- Axes: Count panels in aria-rowcount and offsets, or exclude panels from the paginated places.
- What each answer changes: Count: the count needs the open panels on earlier pages. Exclude: panels carry no aria-rowindex under pagination.
- Answer: open.

### Q28

What accessible name must the group disclosure carry?

- Axes: Name from content (visible label) with state on aria-expanded vs. a message that includes the count vs. keep the verb-led name and accept the count gap.
- What each answer changes: Content naming changes every test that queries 'Expand group X'; a count message changes the message catalog; keeping it closes G5-C07 as declined.
- Answer: Take the accessible name from the content and carry the state on aria-expanded (#3110, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q29

Which right-clicks inside a context surface open its menu: only those whose target lies in the surface's DOM, or also those in a portal that the surface's React children render?

- Axes: DOM containment vs React containment; a portal the wrapped content owns (chart legend overflow) vs a foreign overlay (Dialog, Sheet).
- What each answer changes: DOM containment: inPortaledPanel tests any target the surface does not contain, and the chart legend popover gets the native menu. React containment: the claim drops, and only a documented exception (role dialog) is excluded, if any.
- Answer: open.

### Q30

Does useComboboxQuery belong on the public surface of ui/combobox?

- Axes: Export the hook vs keep it internal.
- What each answer changes: Export: add it to index.ts and its surface index in packages/ui/docs. Internal: the Combobox TSDoc and CONVENTIONS §3.6 mention move to useComboboxDeferredQuery or ui/primitives/query useQuery.
- Answer: open.

### Q31

Must a Select always hold exactly one option, or may it start empty and clear on reclick like a Listbox?

- Axes: Behavior (nullable default false on Select) vs documentation (Select TSDoc sentence).
- What each answer changes: Behavior: Select passes nullable={false} by default, and the docs examples that rely on reclick-clear change. Documentation: the Select TSDoc sentence changes and no code moves.
- Answer: Keep the Listbox nullable default and change the Select TSDoc to match (#3108, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q32

Must every useFormValue control follow a native form reset (the form-reset lens), including SignaturePad and the popover pickers?

- Axes: Yes for all vs only controls that render a native form element.
- What each answer changes: Yes: S100 runs and DatePicker joins it. No: K4-C09/K4-C10 close as intended with a TSDoc line.
- Answer: open.

### Q33

Do Control and RangeSlider, whose `id` goes to a descendant, take a native base, or get NO_BASE_EXEMPT rows?

- Axes: Base with Omit<'id'> and a rest spread on the root vs exemption with reason.
- What each answer changes: Base: breaking-type assent per CLAUDE.md §3.1. Exempt: test-only change.
- Answer: open.

### Q34

Does an explicit `invalid={false}` on Input/Textarea force the state off, or OR with the field and the Control?

- Axes: Override (prop doc, useInputControl) vs OR (Input @remarks).
- What each answer changes: Override: only the @remarks sentence changes. OR: useInputControl line 95 changes, and grid editors that pass `|| undefined` are unaffected.
- Answer: An explicit invalid prop overrides the bound and ambient state; the TSDoc now says so (#3109, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q35

Under forced colors, do the color parts keep their real colors (forced-color-adjust: none) or take sen.forced paints?

- Axes: Real color vs system mark.
- What each answer changes: Real color keeps the chips distinct; a system mark keeps only an edge and the chips stay alike.
- Answer: Paint the real colors under forced colors with forced-color-adjust-none (#3110, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q36

Is a rejected sharp render a failure of the document (land TSDoc, cache.ts:795) or a skip that leaves the page at slot scale (inline comment, cache.ts:812-813)?

- Axes: Error scope for a render after the open: document-wide vs per-raster; applies to sharp renders and possibly thumbnails.
- What each answer changes: Skip: S118 changes land so a sharp rejection adds `index:sharp` to skipped and pumps. Document failure: K5-C06 becomes a comment mismatch, and S118 only aligns the comment.
- Answer: open.

### Q37

Does a (row × column) group whose matching rows hold no numeric value show `emptyCell` or the aggregate of an empty set (0 for count)?

- Axes: What 'match' means for emptyCell: rows grouped by row/column keys vs rows with a numeric value.
- What each answer changes: Aggregate: S123 keeps a bucket for matching rows and shows 0. emptyCell: K6-C08 becomes a TSDoc mismatch, and S123 only aligns the emptyCell TSDoc.
- Answer: open.

### Q38

Inside a Button, should the Button set the size of LoadingDots (and LoadingSpinner) over an explicit `size`, or should they only follow the Button's density scope?

- Axes: Projection owns the slot vs explicit size wins; consistency with REFERENCE.md §2 ("A fixed projection owns its slot").
- What each answer changes: Projection: S124 adds a selector in the button or loading kata. Scope only: K6-C09 is a TSDoc mismatch, and S124 aligns loading-dots.tsx and the button.ts:45-47 comment.
- Answer: open.

### Q39

Does returnFocusTo support an owner that unmounts the alert on its close report?

- Axes: Support it (focus from the unmount path) or document that the alert must stay mounted.
- What each answer changes: Support: S125 covers both rows. Document: S125 keeps only the refused-press reset (K7-C02) and K7-C01 becomes a TSDoc change.
- Answer: open.

### Q40

Must a native text input or select in a Toolbar join the roving set?

- Axes: APG single Tab stop vs APG advice to keep arrow-consuming controls out (or last).
- What each answer changes: Yes: S128 adds them and needs a caret-aware arrow guard. No: S128 adds only checkbox, radio, and switch, and the Toolbar TSDoc names the exclusion.
- Answer: open.

### Q41

What range does a splitter with no maximum expose?

- Axes: Pass a computed max (container or table extent), pass Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, or leave it and accept the ARIA default.
- What each answer changes: A computed max changes the grid handle; MAX_SAFE_INTEGER changes only Splitter; leaving it refutes P1-C03 by decision.
- Answer: Splitter sets aria-valuemax to Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER (#3110, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q42

Where does focus go when the revealed layer of a ReadyReveal has nothing focusable?

- Axes: The activating layer with tabIndex=-1, the root with tabIndex=-1, or nowhere (document the limit).
- What each answer changes: Layer or root adds markup to the primitive; nowhere refutes P1-C05 by a TSDoc change.
- Answer: open.

### Q43

Must the first panel after an empty current set morph the height?

- Axes: Treat it as a panel switch, or as the initial baseline like a mount.
- What each answer changes: Switch: S137 runs. Baseline: P1-C07 is refuted by decision and the latch gets a comment.
- Answer: open.

### Q44

Does the OSRM server the module targets answer NoRoute and NoSegment with HTTP 400 and a JSON body?

- Axes: Verify against osrm-backend's request handler or a live request (this pass could reach neither).
- What each answer changes: Yes (expected): routeFetch reads the body of a 4xx and maps a service code to no-route; the result.ts comment and the map-routing test change. No: M1-C01 is refuted.
- Answer: open.

### Q45

Should any abort of the caller's own signal read as 'aborted', whatever its reason?

- Axes: Classify by the caller's signal state (signal.aborted and not the timeout) against the reason's name.
- What each answer changes: Yes: thrownFailure takes the caller's signal and checks it first. No: the failure.ts TSDoc states that only an AbortError reason reads as 'aborted'.
- Answer: open.

### Q46

In Chrome, Firefox, and Safari, does setting pointer-events none on the zoom layer under a still pointer fire pointerleave on the region group or overlay?

- Axes: Real-browser check with the Playwright tools (CLAUDE.md §3.7): hover a region, shift-wheel without moving, observe the tooltip.
- What each answer changes: Fires: M2-C09 is refuted and S143 drops. Does not fire: S143 runs and M2-C09 becomes CONFIRMED.
- Answer: Chromium fires pointerleave when the zoom layer turns pointer-events off, so M2-C09 does not show there; Firefox and WebKit are unverified. S143 applied anyway: a wheel zoom holds the readout as a pinch does (#3107, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q47

With an explicit colorDomain and no finite value, should resolveValueBins draw the pinned scale or resolve empty?

- Axes: valueExtent's TSDoc (a pinned domain renders a scale) against resolveValueBins' TSDoc (empty when no row is finite).
- What each answer changes: Draw the scale: only the resolveValueBins TSDoc changes. Resolve empty: resolveValueBins returns empty before valueExtent when no value is finite, and a test pins it.
- Answer: open.

### Q48

Should StackedLayout bound its own height so its body scrolls, or does it stay a min-height column whose body scrolls only in a bounded parent?

- Axes: Code change (the layout fills its parent or the viewport) against a TSDoc that names the bounded parent; the admin HeaderShell (apps/admin/app/(signed-in)/client.tsx:147, min-h-dvh) and DashboardLayout depend on the answer.
- What each answer changes: Code: the admin header and the dashboard layout start to scroll their body in place of the page. TSDoc: no behavior change; P2-C12 closes as text.
- Answer: Keep the layout and name the bounded parent in the StackedLayout TSDoc (#3108, low-row thread, 2026-10-30).

### Q49

After its last row goes, does the summary stay closed until the next failed submit?

- Axes: Live list after the first failure (today) against closed until the next failure (as a reset does).
- What each answer changes: Live list: Q2-C09 becomes intended, and the TSDoc can say so. Closed: S168 runs and the reset rule and the last-row rule match.
- Answer: open.

### Q50

Does sky follow the one-rung-stronger rule of the palette?

- Axes: Change the light soft, outline, and plain sky text to sky-800, against keep sky-700 and name the exception.
- What each answer changes: sky-800: a visible shade change in each soft, outline, and plain sky Badge and Button. Exception: a TSDoc sentence only.
- Answer: open.

## Ruled out

- **C1-C02** (`modules/chart/engine/chart-time.ts`, dateCategoryFormat): Every value matches the dotted-date shape and parses day-first with a two-digit year (`1.2.0` reads as 1 February 2000), so the axis, tooltip, and data table relabel the versions as dates such as `02/01/2000`. Ruled out: chart-time.ts:81-82 "A year of one or two digits reads as `Date.parse` reads it: below 50 in the 2000s, else in the 1900s."
- **C1-C08** (`modules/chart/engine/chart-cartesian/series.ts`, orderReadout): The tooltip lists the stacked series by their latest value instead of in series order, so its rows no longer read in the left-to-right order of the stack segments. Ruled out: use-chart-cartesian.ts:107-108 "Off (the default) keeps the series order, which the drawn order of grouped bars and stacks must match." with line-chart.tsx:76 as the only legendByValue caller.
- **C1-C09** (`modules/chart/engine/chart-geometry/pie.ts`, pieCalloutFit): The radius shrinks to leave room for the longest label of a row that takes no slice and draws no callout, so the pie draws smaller than the frame allows. Ruled out: sector-chart-callouts.tsx:81 "share == null ? '' : calloutLabelText(spec, index, share),"
- **C2-C04** (`modules/chart/engine/chart-legend/legend.tsx`, ChartLegendReferenceSwitch): Each chip still renders as a focusable toggle button with aria-pressed="true" and a click handler. A press does not toggle anything, but it still writes the reference emphasis. Assistive tech announces a toggle that does not toggle. Ruled out: engine/chart-frame/cartesian.tsx:206-210 is the only caller that passes references, and it passes onToggleReference={chart.toggleReference}.
- **C2-C12** (`modules/chart/engine/chart-context-menu.tsx`, ChartContextMenu (fullscreen dialog content)): The chart copy unmounts as soon as open goes false, while the dialog still runs its exit animation. The auto-height panel collapses to its footer and jumps during the exit (on mobile the sheet drops to the height of the Close row). Ruled out: chart-context-menu.tsx:385-386 "its legend starts with nothing hidden and is destroyed on close"; the quoted rule at 360-365 governs the Dialog element only.
- **C2-C13** (`modules/chart/engine/chart-data-dialog.tsx`, ChartDataDialog): The table unmounts in the render that starts the close, so the panel shrinks to its title and footer during the exit animation. Ruled out: chart-data-dialog.tsx:44-45 "The dialog builds the table only while it is open."
- **C3-C03** (`modules/chart/area-chart/area-chart.tsx`, AreaChart (crosshair default, stacked)): The default snapping rule does not meet a band-edge point and does not carry the tooltip there: the stacked chart hands no value snap points, so the tooltip floats at the pointer height and nothing isolates off the marks. Ruled out: area-chart.tsx:319-321 "Above the fills the emphasis goes to the band edge the tooltip anchors in the snapped column. A stacked column offers no stops, so its readout floats free and nothing isolates outside the stack." The wording of the AreaChartProps.crosshair TSDoc is recorded as a lead.
- **D1-C05** (`modules/dashboard/engine/dashboard-layout.ts`, mergeLayout / resolveLayout): The moved tile and the returning tile are saved on the same cells, and both render on top of each other. Nothing moves either one, because an entry that the clamp does not move keeps its place also when it overlaps. Ruled out: dashboard-layout.ts:544-545 "An entry that the clamp does not move keeps its place, also when it overlaps another entry as saved." Test dashboard-handle.test.tsx:103-127 pins the tidy result over the unmounted entry. No quoted rule says that tiles never overlap on mount.
- **G2-C02** (`modules/grid/use-grid-column-sizing.ts`, useGridColumnSizing (commit / resetWidths)): onValueChange receives the full set of freshly fitted widths instead of {}. The consumer persists them, so the next mount seeds them and starts in manual mode; and because the automatic fit stands down under a controlled binding, a later container resize does not refit them. The widths are not given back to the automatic fit. Ruled out: use-grid-column-sizing.ts:362-364 "Under a controlled binding the automatic fit writes no data width, and every other write reports its widths, because the consumer owns the state." A controlled `{}` would leave the columns unfitted, since the fit stands down under a controlled binding.
- **G2-C05** (`modules/grid/use-grid-cursor.tsx`, isPlainKey): The browser-modifier test is a hand copy of the shared home, so a change to the home's rule does not reach the grid's type-to-edit and F2 entry. Ruled out: browser-modifier-boundary.test.ts:18-22 "Files that keep a hand-written chain, each with the reason." lists use-grid-cursor.tsx with its reason; CONVENTIONS.md §3.10 names that gate as the holder of the home.
- **G2-C06** (`modules/grid/grid-nav-cell.tsx`, GridNavCell (reveal effect)): The cursor follows its cell to the new row (aria-activedescendant moves), but the cell does not scroll into view and is not cleared of the sticky chrome; the active cell is off screen. The pending reveal is then claimed later by whichever cell next mounts active, which can scroll a cell that a window scroll brings back. Ruled out: The followed cell's active flag flips false then true across the sort render and the reconcile render, so grid-nav-cell.tsx:107 `if (active && store.claimReveal())` runs for the new change; probe shows scrollIntoView on the followed cell.
- **G2-C09** (`modules/grid/grid-context-menu-utilities.tsx`, sortMenuItems): The Sort menu offers "Sort ascending" for column A although the column already holds that direction. Ruled out: grid-context-menu-utilities.tsx:102-103 "Under a multi-column sort either direction still collapses the sort onto this column, and both stay on offer."
- **G3-C03** (`modules/grid/use-grid-table.ts`, useGridTable (onPaginationChange) / buildPaginationView.setPageIndex): pagination.onValueChange fires with the same pageIndex and pageSize. A server-paginated consumer refetches the page it already shows. Ruled out: TanStack's setStateSlice returns the current slice for a structural no-op: utils.js:151 'return isEqual(current, next) ? current : next;'. The grid's setter then resolves to prev, and useControllable drops it: use-controllable.ts:110 'if (Object.is(normalized, base) && Object.is(normalized, heldRef.current)) return'. A report fires only for a controlled owner that passes a new object each render (identity differs from the last report), which use-controllable.ts:36-41 documents as intended ('An owner that shows a value other than the value it holds gets the report that moves its state back'); no root does that.
- **G3-C05** (`modules/grid/grid-data-resolvers.ts`, resolveFooterStats): The footer reads the server total ('1,000 rows') while only the few matching rows show; it never reads 'N of M rows visible'. The selected label's denominator is also the server total. Ruled out: resolveFooterStats TSDoc states the precedence on purpose: 'An infinite-scroll `totalRows` supersedes the filtered extent'. See leads for the wording conflict with GridFooter.rowTotal.
- **G3-C06** (`modules/grid/grid-data-resolvers.ts`, resolveAriaRowCount): aria-rowcount advertises totalRows + 1 although the grid shows only the filtered rows, so assistive tech reports a row set that the filter does not hold. Ruled out: TSDoc rule: 'The server total + 1 when ... infinite-scrolling with a known {@link GridInfiniteScroll.totalRows}.' The claim's quoted rules do not say what the claim says (§3.7).
- **G3-C07** (`modules/grid/grid-busy-status.tsx`, GridBusyStatus / useStatusMessage): About 150 ms after mount each grid's live region speaks 'N rows', although no load started and no count changed. The pagination footer's status is kept silent on its initial render for this reason. Ruled out: grid-interaction.test.tsx:529-535 asserts the mount-time count announcement; the pagination footer's silent initial render is a separate region with a separate rule.
- **G4-C06** (`modules/grid/engine/grid-row/bridges.ts`, bridgeRowActivate / buildRovingCellActivate): The handler receives an event whose currentTarget is the <table> or the <td>, not the <tr> that the GridRowClick type promises, so row reads give undefined or wrong values. Ruled out: bridges.ts:124-126 '...the event's element type is widened to the row-click's `<tr>` signature, as the cursor bridge does.' The public GridRowClick TSDoc does not warn the consumer; recorded as a lead.
- **G5-C06** (`modules/grid/engine/grid-aggregate.ts`, aggregateLabelSpan): The first column's aggregate is never rendered on the group header, the group total, or the grand total: its cell holds the disclosure button or the "Total" label instead, and GridAggregateCells starts at column index 1. Ruled out: grid-aggregate.ts:138-140 "The span is at least one, so the label always has a cell, even when the first column aggregates." plus the test at grid-aggregation.test.tsx:139-140 make the label win over a leading aggregate on purpose.
- **G5-C09** (`modules/grid/engine/grid-export/excel.ts`, sheetCell / rowsToXlsx): The workbook carries an inline string past Excel's per-cell limit of 32,767 characters, so Excel reports unreadable content and offers to repair the file, which drops or truncates the content. Ruled out: The quoted TSDoc describes the format change from HTML .xls, not a cap on cell length; the 32,767 limit is an Excel application limit, not an OOXML validity rule, so no written rule says what the claim says.
- **G5-C11** (`modules/grid/engine/grid-export/csv.ts`, rowsToCsv): The CSV cell is written as "'-1,234.50": a leading apostrophe that a spreadsheet shows as part of the text, so the column no longer parses as numbers, although the grid sorts and aggregates the same value as a number. Ruled out: export-output.ts:27-31 and :58-60 document that a signed non-plain field gets the quote prefix unless the caller opts into `formatted`; the grid passes consumer text it cannot vouch for, and the opt-in is a chart-only choice (chart-export.ts:659).
- **H1-C05** (`hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts`, setVirtualActive): Arrow moves shift `data-active` but never write `aria-selected`, so no item reads as selected. A row that the pointer path marked `aria-selected="true"` keeps it after the arrow moves the highlight away, so the stale row stays selected for assistive technology. Ruled out: use-a11y-roving.ts:1249-1251 makes the aria-selected mirror conditional on activeDescendantRef ("When provided ..."), and no caller constructs virtual mode without it.
- **H1-C11** (`hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts`, useA11yRoving (focusVirtualIndexed)): The mount watcher stays connected with no later roving move to cancel it. When the row mounts later (a scroll, a re-render), focus jumps back into the list and away from where the reader is. Ruled out: The quoted TSDoc names a later roving move as what supersedes a target; it does not promise a disconnect on focus leaving. The scrollToIndex call at :376 mounts the row on the virtualizer's next render, so a reader cannot move focus away inside that gap; the getKey-mismatch variant never focuses anything.
- **H1-C12** (`hooks/a11y/use-typeahead.ts`, isTypeaheadKey / useTypeahead): The space never joins the buffer, so the query becomes "newy", which matches no item; the highlight stays on "New Jersey" and "New York" cannot be reached by type-ahead. Ruled out: use-typeahead.ts:13 excludes Space explicitly, and Space is the activation key of a menu (use-a11y-roving.ts:1222-1227) and of a listbox option; the quoted RovingOptions.typeahead TSDoc does not say a space joins the buffer.
- **K2-C01** (`components/listbox/listbox.tsx`, Listbox): No clear button renders. The custom suffix takes the whole suffix slot, so the reader has no way to clear the selection from the trigger. Ruled out: __tests__/components/listbox.test.tsx:227 it('gives the suffix slot to a custom suffix over the clear button, as Combobox does' ... :241 expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Clear selection' })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
- **K2-C05** (`components/date-picker/use-date-picker-keyboard.ts`, useDatePickerKeyboard (handleGridKey)): Nothing happens: the highlight stays on the same day. A standalone Calendar moves on Home/End, but the picker's grid zone handles only the arrows, the Page keys, Enter, and Space. Ruled out: date-picker.tsx:258-259 "routes arrow/Page keys to the active zone"; calendar.tsx Home/End sentence is under "With no `active`", which the picker never meets; digest R10 records the Home/End exclusion as an owner decision.
- **K2-C10** (`components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts`, useComboboxInput (onChange) / Combobox `clearOnEmpty`): The selection stays. `clearOnEmpty` does nothing in `multiple` mode. Ruled out: __tests__/components/use-combobox-input.test.ts:117 it('does not clear in multi-select mode' ... :128 expect(setValue).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
- **K3-C01** (`components/number-input/number-input.tsx`, NumberInput): The text input shows read-only (the Form lock reaches it through useControlProps), but both steppers stay enabled. For a controlled or unbound field the press changes the value and fires onValueChange during the submit; for a bound field the write is refused, but the new number is still announced through the live region. Ruled out: button.tsx:163 `{...(locked && lockedProps)}` after the props spread replaces the stepper onClick with cancelActivation (button-constants.ts:7-11 `event.preventDefault()` / `event.stopPropagation()`); the stepper shows aria-disabled/data-disabled, the documented lock state.
- **K3-C14** (`components/fieldset/message.tsx`, Message): The message renders an empty <p role="status"> and registers its id, so the input's aria-describedby points at an empty description. An error message with the same children renders nothing and registers nothing. Ruled out: message.tsx:144-145 "warning/success always render their children" states the behavior on purpose, and the falsy-children rule that the claim quotes (shouldRenderError) applies to the error severity only.
- **K4-C08** (`components/color/color-hex-input.tsx`, ColorHexInput): At the sixth digit the field commits a 6-digit hex, which sets the alpha to 100% and emits an opaque value through onValueChange; the alpha comes back only at the eighth digit (and is lost if the reader stops at six). Ruled out: color-hex-input.tsx:21 "A 6 or 8 digit hex commits as the reader types it": a 6-digit hex is a full hex by the TSDoc, so the opaque commit at the sixth digit is intended; the comment at :46 holds back only a 3 or 4 digit draft.
- **K4-C13** (`components/color/color-swatches.tsx`, ColorSwatches): The checked chip looks the same as every other chip; only the focus outline (when the radio has keyboard focus) and assistive technology show which chip is checked. Ruled out: Neither quoted rule says what the claim says: the TSDoc promises the checked radio, which color-swatches.tsx:98 keeps, and §5.10 is scoped to forced colors. See leads.
- **K5-C07** (`components/pdf-viewer/use-pdf-viewer.ts`, nextReport / usePdfViewer report effect): The first viewer calls `onError` and then `onLoad` for the same `src`. Ruled out: Deliberate: use-pdf-viewer.ts:420-424 "This viewer, still subscribed, must report the success that retry produces." The error-then-load sequence is the designed report. The public onError sentence "Exactly one of `onLoad` and `onError` fires for each `src`" (pdf-viewer.tsx:78-79) does not match that design; recorded as a lead.
- **K6-C02** (`components/list/list.tsx`, List): Each toggle remounts the whole list: the `<ul>` changes between the Motion `Reorder.Group` and a plain `<ul>`, and each row changes between `ListItemReorder` and `ListItemStatic` at the same key. Uncontrolled state inside the rows (a checkbox, an input draft) resets, and focus inside a row drops to the document body. Ruled out: The quoted contract does not say what the claim says: "Disables all drag and keyboard reorder interaction" states what the prop does, and "A focusable child, such as a checkbox, keeps its own stop" (list-item.tsx:98) is about Tab stops, not state across a toggle. The claim argues from silence ("nothing states that it recreates the rows"), which §3.7 refutes.
- **K6-C05** (`components/avatar/avatar.tsx`, Avatar): Assistive technology still reads the status name ("Active") from the sr-only span, so a decorative avatar exposes a stray word with no subject. Ruled out: Deliberate: avatar.tsx:221 "Color alone conveys status; the sr-only span names it for assistive technology." The status text exists because color alone carries the status, apart from the image name that `alt` controls; statusLabel has its own default and opt-out.
- **K7-C03** (`components/alert/alert.tsx`, Alert): The rendered root `<div>` has no `role`, or the role of the severity. The caller's `role` is dropped without a warning. Ruled out: CONVENTIONS.md §3.9: "The load-bearing structural attributes come after `{...props}`: `role`, `tabIndex`, `type` ... Then a stray prop cannot drop a row out of roving, turn a button into a form submit, or clobber a bound field."
- **M1-C12** (`modules/map/engine/map-zip/selection.ts`, readRule (parseZipSelection)): The term reads as the ZIP+4 of 60601, so the territory covers the one code 60601 and nothing else of the span; the term is not marked invalid either Ruled out: selection.ts:45 "Read before {@link RANGE_TERM}, which this shape also satisfies." and selection.ts:126 "Read first, because a range would read this shape as a span as well." The precedence covers every 5-4 term, forward or backward.
- **M1-C13** (`modules/map/engine/map-region/category.ts`, deriveCategories / regionCategoryIndexes): String(null) and String(undefined) become categories 'null' and 'undefined': they take palette slots, list in the legend as 'null'/'undefined', and paint their regions in a slot color instead of the neutral no-data fill Ruled out: category.ts:183-185 "`null` where no row or no listed category matches: the region draws in the neutral no-data fill." A derived 'null' is a listed category; the quoted blank-cell rule is the numeric branch's, not the categorical one's.
- **P1-C09** (`primitives/panel/panel-trigger.tsx`, PanelTrigger): The trigger's classes are dropped; the clone carries only the child's classes. Ruled out: panel-trigger.tsx:33: "The props of the child win a clash, and a handler on both runs the two." (className is not excepted). See lead on PopoverTrigger, which documents a merge instead.
- **P2-C09** (`layouts/sidebar/sidebar.tsx`, SidebarLayout (floating peek)): The floating sheet opens only on `pointerenter` of an `aria-hidden` hot zone; the inline panel is hidden. No key opens the peek, so the navigation is unreachable from the keyboard unless the user knows ⌘B, which changes the persistent setting and does nothing while focus is in a form field. Ruled out: The TSDoc documents the peek as pointer-only (sidebar.tsx:123-125) and documents ⌘B as the keyboard path that shows the sidebar (sidebar.tsx:140-142, handler at :207). A key that restores the inline panel satisfies the keyboard operation the claim asks for; a press in a form field is skipped by design (sidebar.tsx:76-77 "It also skips a keydown during an IME composition and a press in a form field."), and focus can leave the field first.
- **P2-C10** (`utilities/keyboard-navigation.ts`, nextIndexForKey): ArrowRight always gives index + 1, which in RTL moves focus to the item on the left. The function takes no direction, so a caller cannot get the mirrored result from it. Ruled out: CONVENTIONS.md §3.10 assigns the RTL swap to logicalArrowKey, which useA11yRoving applies (use-a11y-roving.ts:1467) before nextIndexForKey; a direction argument here would be the second copy the convention forbids.
- **Q1-C03** (`modules/query/engine/query-node.ts`, createRule): The new rule already holds the first option and filters the rows to it; `isQueryActive` gives true, the summary and the chips show `Status is Active`, though the user set nothing. Ruled out: defaultValueFor TSDoc (query-node.ts:47-49) states the select seed is the first option, and query-node.test.ts:52 pins it; the general isQueryActive sentence does not override the specific documented seed.
- **Q1-C04** (`modules/query/engine/query-node.ts`, createRule): The new rule takes the operator `isTrue`, which needs no value, so it filters to the rows that are true at once; `isQueryActive` is true and the summary shows `Active is true` for a freshly seeded builder. Ruled out: createRule TSDoc (query-node.ts:14-15) sets the operator to the field's first (isTrue for boolean, query-operators.ts:142), isQueryActive TSDoc (query-active.ts:18-19) reads a value-less operator as active, and query-builder.test.tsx:324-334 pins the 'Is true' seed.
- **Q1-C13** (`modules/events/events-aside.tsx`, EventsAside): The store is paused for the whole panel load, so every app event of that window (input, scroll, paint, network, route) is dropped from the list and from later bug trails. Ruled out: events-aside.tsx:50-51 states the pause during the panel load on purpose; the TSDoc sentence "the panel does not pause the store" (events-aside.tsx:32-33) speaks of the mounted panel, against the sheet that holds the pause while open (events-sheet.tsx:57-58). Bugs still record during the pause (events-store.ts:146).
- **Q2-C06** (`modules/form/form-filters/form-filters.tsx`, FormFilters): The record is set as-is, so `onValueChange` reports and the context `value` holds the empty keys (`status: ''`, `tags: []`) that the bar otherwise drops; the payload is not minimal after a clear or on mount. Ruled out: form-filters.test.tsx:521 + :543 assert that a clear reports `defaultValue` as given, empty `name: ''` included; TSDoc form-filters.tsx:79 states that a clear restores `defaultValue`.
- **R1-C01** (`recipes/kiso/kokkaku/heading.ts`, heading.ramp): The skeleton bar is shorter than the real heading line at each level: about 32px vs a 36px text-3xl line at level 1, 20px vs a 28px text-lg line at level 4, 16px vs a 24px line at level 5, and 12px vs a 20px text-sm line at level 6. The layout shifts when the content loads. Ruled out: ready-reveal-shift.test.tsx:12-13 documents the shorter bar as the design ("sized to its font, standing shorter than the real `Heading`'s line box"), and CONVENTIONS §3.7 makes ReadyReveal hold the box, so the swap does not shift.
- **R1-C06** (`recipes/kiso/kokkaku/date-picker.ts`, datePicker.icon): The comment says the trigger icon is in a slot scope one step below the trigger, but the skeleton icon uses dan.size.icon.base at the host step; kata/date-picker.ts says the icon is inside the button and not in a suffix slot. Either the comment is false or the skeleton icon span is one step (2px) larger than the real icon. Ruled out: date-picker-skeleton.tsx:39 writes data-density="slot" on the icon span, as date-picker-trigger.tsx:197 does around the real icon, so the comment holds and the two icons have one size.
- **R1-C13** (`recipes/kata/tabs.ts`, segment.indicator): The sliding indicator is a background only, which forced colors remove, and the selected item's stronger ink also maps to CanvasText; the selected item has no visible mark. Ruled out: active-indicator.ts:10 adds sen.forced.outline to every ActiveIndicator, and active-indicator.tsx:123 merges it under the segment indicator class, so the selected segment keeps an outline; forced-colors-indicators.test.tsx:399 asserts it.
- **R1-C14** (`recipes/kata/stepper.ts`, k.indicator): The indicator shows completed (green), current (blue fill), and upcoming (zinc) only by background; forced colors remove the fills, so the current step and an upcoming step look the same (both a number with no circle). Ruled out: The current step's fill is an ActiveIndicator (stepper-indicator.tsx:35), which carries sen.forced.outline (active-indicator.ts:10), so the current step keeps a circle edge that an upcoming step lacks; a completed step keeps its check glyph.
- **R1-C18** (`recipes/kata/grid.ts`, k cell.mark): The amber highlight is a background only; forced colors remove it, so the matches are not marked. Ruled out: grid-highlight-utilities.tsx:94 renders a <mark>, and forced colors keep the system Mark color on it (Chromium probe), so a match stays marked. A separate dark-mode contrast lead is under leads.
- **R1-C22** (`recipes/kiso/hannou/fg.ts`, fg.disabled): The label is not dimmed, because the rule reads only `has-disabled` (:disabled). Ruled out: The quoted rule "disabled lifts it" does not say data-disabled, and that other hannou rules gate on both is a pattern, not a written rule. Each control of the field row writes native `disabled` (switch.tsx:140, radio.tsx:160, checkbox.tsx:146), so `has-disabled` matches.
- **R1-C23** (`recipes/kata/tree.ts`, check): The comment says a checked or mixed box paints in Highlight under forced colors, but every fill rule keys only on `data-checked`; a mixed box that carries a separate attribute (such as data-indeterminate, which kumi/check uses) gets no fill and no forced Highlight. Ruled out: tree-item-content.tsx:157 stamps data-checked for `checked !== false`, which includes 'mixed', so the fill and the forced Highlight apply; forced-colors-indicators.test.tsx:591 asserts it.
- **R1-C24** (`recipes/kata/textarea.ts`, k.actions (slot)): The actions row inset stays a fixed 8px (`pe-2 pb-2`) while its gap and the control padding step, so the actions sit off the field's text inset at other steps. Ruled out: The quoted §5.5 does not say what the claim says: it states where stepped values live, not that a fixed padding is a defect, and the kata gives no alignment contract for the row.

Parts that a NARROWED verdict drops are under each Mechanisms entry as "Dropped part".

## Surfaced, not judged

- `modules/chart/engine/chart-legend/range-legend.tsx` (sweeper C3): RangeLegend clears its probe only on pointerleave and blur; it has no unmount cleanup that calls onProbe(null), so a host that unmounts it mid-probe keeps the last probe (the heatmap dim in C3-C01; check the choropleth MapRangeLegend path too).
- `modules/grid/engine/grid-row/cell.ts` (sweeper G2): INTERACTIVE_CELL_CONTENT matches only [contenteditable="true"]; an editor with contenteditable="" or contenteditable="plaintext-only" is not seen as interactive, so a press in it seats the cursor and focuses the grid (seatingCellProps, useGridNavStopProps). grid-menu-targeting.ts TEXT_FIELD uses [contenteditable]:not([contenteditable="false"]) instead.
- `modules/grid/use-grid-table.ts` (sweeper G2): rowsSignature = hashKeys(rowKeys); if rowKeys under client grouping hold every leaf, expanding a collapsed group does not change it, so the column sizing does not re-measure newly shown leaves (see G2-C07).
- `modules/grid/use-grid-client-view.ts` (sweeper G3): useFilterModeMismatchWarning returns early unless both surfaces are configured, so a manual flag on an unconfigured surface that silences the other surface warns nothing (see G3-C04 mechanism).
- `modules/grid/grid-column-filter-button.tsx` (sweeper G3): apply() calls filter.setQuery even when the draft equals the applied query, which reports an unchanged filter and resets the page (see G3-C02).
- `modules/grid/use-grid-roving.ts` (sweeper G4): rowStaticStop is `onRowClick && virtualized && !navigable`, and roving mode is 'none' when virtualized. A virtualized, non-navigable grid with only onCellClick, onRowDoubleClick, or onCellDoubleClick gives rows the clickable focus ring but no Tab stop and no roving, so a keyboard cannot reach the click action.
- `modules/grid/grid-data-resolvers.ts` (sweeper G4): resolveAriaRowCount uses pagination.rowCount + 1 when paginating and ignores open detail panels, while the body gives each open panel an aria-rowindex place (see G4-C07); the count and the page offset (resolveSemantics rowOffset) leave out panels.
- `components/control/use-control-picker-popover.ts` (sweeper K2): `onOpen`/`onClose` and `setTouched` run only inside `openPicker`/`closePicker`. A controlled `open` prop that flips without those calls skips the variant's open/close side effects (DatePicker range `resetSelection`, single `clearActive`) and the touched mark; ColorPicker shares the hook.
- `hooks/use-controllable.ts` (sweeper K3): useControllable keeps the newest onValueChange in a ref that a layout effect writes (onValueChangeRef); CONVENTIONS §10.8 says not to keep the newest handler in a ref that an effect writes. Its comment gives a render-phase reason; check against the rule.
- `__tests__/boundary/props-base-boundary.test.ts` (sweeper K3): RatingProps, RangeSliderProps, ControlProps, and SegmentItemProps take no native base and are not in NO_BASE_EXEMPT; if the gate passes, its detection misses them.
- `components/control/use-control-picker-field.ts` (sweeper K4): Discards the resetRef of useFormValue, so every popover picker (ColorPicker, DatePicker) ignores a native form reset while uncontrolled.
- `recipes/kata/color-panel.ts` (sweeper K4): Swatch, area, hue/alpha track and preview carry no forced-color-adjust or forced fallback; the swatch slot has no checked style.
- `recipes/kata/progress.ts` (sweeper K6): The gauge arc (`k.color[color].stroke`, from `iro/meter.ts`) and the gauge track carry no forced-colors fallback, unlike the bar fill (`forced.mark`) and track (`forced.outline`). Under forced colors, if the engine forces SVG stroke, the arc and the track paint one system color and the value is not visible (CONVENTIONS.md §5.10: "A fill or a bar that shows a value or a selection takes `mark`"). Sparkline strokes and fills (`iro/meter.ts`) have the same gap.
- `recipes/kata/button.ts` (sweeper K6): The base comment says a LoadingSpinner, LoadingDots, or Kbd child "sizes itself in a button: its own recipe selects a parent with `data-variant`... See `kata/loading.ts`"; `kata/loading.ts` has no such selector (only `kata/kbd.ts` does). Stale comment, see K6-C09.
- `utilities/color-scale.ts` (sweeper M1): resolveColorBins with a non-integer bins (e.g. a map's bins={2.5}): binColors builds Array.from({length: 2.5}) = 2 bins, but step is span/2.5 and the last-bin test `bin === count - 1` never holds, so the last legend bin ends at 80% of the span (not max) while binIndex assigns by linear.length = 2 halves. Legend labels and region fills disagree.
- `modules/map/engine/map-overlay/readout.ts` (sweeper M2): markRows falls back to mark.kind ('point', 'route', 'geofence', 'marker') as the table value of a mark with no detail. That string is an internal identifier shown to screen-reader users in English, outside the intl message catalog.
- `modules/map/engine/map-cluster/crowd.ts` (sweeper M2): The header records that the plural marks do not yet divide ground against the pool, which contradicts the pool contract in context.ts (see M2-C05, M2-C06).
- `recipes/kata/box.ts` (sweeper P2): p, px, and py are three independent responsive axes on overlapping padding properties; a prefixed p class overrides an unprefixed px/py class at its breakpoint (see the Box claim).
- `recipes/kata/sidebar-layout.ts` (sweeper P2): floating.sheet has no layout axis, so the floating Sheet shows in the drawer layout when its open state stays true.
- `hooks/use-reorder-drag.ts` (sweeper Q1): The Escape listener for a live drag is a window capture listener that calls preventDefault but not stopPropagation; inside a Dialog or Sheet, an Escape that cancels a QueryBuilder drag may also close the overlay. Not verified against the overlay's Escape handler.
- `hooks/use-keyboard-reorder.ts` (sweeper Q1): Escape that drops a keyboard lift calls preventDefault only; in an overlay the same Escape may also dismiss the overlay. Not verified.
- `HeadingSkeleton component (packages/ui/src, outside R1)` (sweeper R1): Confirm that it uses kokkaku.heading.ramp[level] as the bar height (R1-C01).
- `DatePicker component (packages/ui/src, outside R1)` (sweeper R1): Confirm whether the trigger icon is in a slot scope one step down; decides R1-C06.
- `Tree component (packages/ui/src, outside R1)` (sweeper R1): Check which attribute the check box gets for aria-checked=mixed; decides R1-C23.
- `Grid search highlight component (outside R1)` (sweeper R1): Check whether it renders a <mark>; the kata sets text-inherit and a bg-only wash with no forced fallback (R1-C18).
- `modules/chart/scatter-chart/scatter-chart-chrome.tsx` (verifier V1): ScatterChartChrome draws the gridlines on xGrid/yGrid while `axes` gates only the axes and titles, so scatter axes={false} keeps gridlines too; the twin of C1-C10 (surfaced, not judged).
- `modules/chart/engine/chart-legend/legend.tsx` (verifier V1): ChartLegendOverflowSwitch and ChartLegendReferenceSwitch gate pointerenter on touch but call emphasis.point(null) on every pointerleave; the series switch path (legend-switch.tsx onPointerLeave) is the same. Not judged beyond C2-C03.
- `modules/chart/engine/chart-frame/frame.tsx` (verifier V1): At spark, labelledBy is dropped (`!spark && heading && title`), so a titled spark figure loses its aria-labelledby to the title; cross-check with the veil intent of C2-C07 (surfaced, not judged).
- `modules/chart/engine/chart-context-menu.tsx` (verifier V1): C2-C12 and C2-C13 were refuted on documented intent, but both dialogs shrink to their chrome during the exit animation; an owner who wants the exit to keep its content would change the documented gate in both (surfaced, not judged).
- `modules/chart/engine/chart-legend/range-legend.tsx` (verifier V2): Cross-component seam: RangeLegend has no unmount cleanup for onProbe, and the choropleth reaches it through MapRangeLegend; its region emphasis may stay after the legend unmounts. Not judged.
- `modules/chart/area-chart/area-chart.tsx` (verifier V2): Adjacent: AreaChartProps.crosshair TSDoc (:27-31) says the default rule "meets the nearest band-edge point and carries the tooltip there" and "Stacked ribbons ... keep the snap", while tooltipSnapPoints (:66-75) floats the stacked tooltip. - `modules/dashboard/engine/dashboard-layout.ts` (verifier V2): Adjacent: minColumns (:249-253) adds a gap for any minWidth, so a JSX DashboardTile with minWidth={0} starves the same way as the D1-C01 spec tile. Not judged.

- `modules/grid/types.ts:397-398` (verifier V3): GridColumnSizing remarks say "\"Reset column widths\" fires it with `{}`" without the controlled exception that use-grid-column-sizing.ts:362-364 records (a controlled reset reports widths). TSDoc and code disagree; not judged.
